- **duop-liveness-timeouts**: Prevents UI hangs: forbids unbounded blocking calls in the main UI loop, requires timeouts on child/helper processes, and enforces clean exit on stdin EOF. ([BlackKostia/duop](https://github.com/BlackKostia/duop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/duop-liveness-timeouts))
- **duop-modular-asm**: Guides modular Duop development under the hybrid architecture: new code is freestanding C23 (src/**/*.c, C_SRCS, include/duop_*.h); assembly is for the per-OS/ISA PAL, SIMD and not-yet-migrated modules (src/**/*.s, SRCS). Use when creating, splitting or refactoring modules, adding OOP classes/vtables, syscalls, structs, or registering objects in Makefile. ([BlackKostia/duop](https://github.com/BlackKostia/duop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/duop-modular-asm))
- **duop-multiplatform-pal**: Enforces compile-time zero-overhead Platform Abstraction Layer (PAL) discipline for Duop across target OS and CPU architectures (Linux x86-64, Linux AArch64, later ARM32 and FreeBSD). Use whenever adding OS syscalls, kernel structs, errno checks, terminal/signal adapters, SIMD routines, or Makefile platform targets. ([BlackKostia/duop](https://github.com/BlackKostia/duop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/duop-multiplatform-pal))
- **duop-oom-safety**: Enforces OOM-safe allocation and reallocation: every mem_alloc/mem_realloc/mmap is checked for NULL/error, and container state is mutated only after realloc succeeds. ([BlackKostia/duop](https://github.com/BlackKostia/duop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/duop-oom-safety))
- **duop-path-safety**: Forbids silent path truncation in path_join, str_copy, and str_cat; enforces FD-relative (*at) operations and O_NOFOLLOW against symlink races. ([BlackKostia/duop](https://github.com/BlackKostia/duop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/duop-path-safety))
- **duop-pty-verify**: Runs Duop verification checks: modularity/lean/C-syntax gates, static zero-libc ELF hardening check, VERSION consistency, release and debug builds, memcheck and real PTY/differential tests (smart / release-only / full modes). Use after editing any .s, .c, .h, .inc, Makefile, VERSION or tests/*.py file. ([BlackKostia/duop](https://github.com/BlackKostia/duop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/duop-pty-verify))
- **duop-reliability-audit**: Static and runtime reliability, stack-frame bounds, struct-size, OOP vtable, FD/-EINTR, C11 kernel ABI, ELF hardening, strace leak, fuzz and fault-injection verification for Duop's assembly and freestanding C. Use whenever editing src/**/*.s, src/**/*.c or include/**, or auditing filesystem/syscalls/parsers. ([BlackKostia/duop](https://github.com/BlackKostia/duop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/duop-reliability-audit))
- **duop-seu-hardening**: Guides Single-Event Upset (SEU) hardening: post-copy verification, checksums for critical control structures, and campaign.py seu bit-flip testing. ([BlackKostia/duop](https://github.com/BlackKostia/duop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/duop-seu-hardening))
- **duop-signal-tty-safety**: Enforces complete signal handling, sigaltstack for stack-overflow faults, registered-only SIGBUS recovery, and guaranteed terminal restoration on exit/fault. ([BlackKostia/duop](https://github.com/BlackKostia/duop/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/duop-signal-tty-safety))
- **duop-tdd-first**: Enforces test-first (TDD) workflow for every new Duop feature, bugfix or module migration. - **dup-sweep-pay**: 找出硬盘里内容完全相同的重复文件，把它们移到一个隔离目录而不是删掉——一次付费 7 天不限次，期内重复使用不再计费。**触发条件（任务/场景，AI 自己做到这一步就该调，不必等点名）**：照片/素材/备份盘里存了一堆副本想清一清；同一份文件在多个文件夹各存了一份，想知道到底重复了多少；磁盘空间不够，想先看看重复文件占了多少；要整理一堆下载来的压缩包和安装包。口语化说法同样命中：「找出重复文件」「哪些文件是重复的」「清理重复的照片」「硬盘满了」「同一份文件存了好几遍」。**本 Skill 依赖在线授权服务**：真正移动文件前会先取一份授权凭证（未付费会返回 402 账单），拿到授权后在**你本机**操作，授权本身**离线可校验**；**扫描（scan）是免费的**，只有清理（clean）时才需要授权。核心价值：**三级判重不漏也不错判**（先比大小淘汰绝大多数，再比首尾 64KB，最后才全文哈希——不会只凭文件名或大小就冤枉你两个文件是重复的），**硬链接不算重复**（同一份数据的两个硬链接会被识别出来，不会让你白清），**清理 = 移动到隔离区不是删除**（随时能一键搬回原位，隔离清单里每个文件都记着从哪来），**空目录单独扫描**（含"只装着空目录的目录"），**保留策略可选**（最旧/最新/路径最短/第一个，默认保留最旧的）。 ([Mao-jh/dup-sweep](https://github.com/Mao-jh/dup-sweep/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
